{"parentUuid":"ec9b9fc9-3674-4d4c-b534-19561f28140c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014TzfFrYJ7aPt1GPfD4QwX9","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[Forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[WindHaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[WindHaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[Lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[Dawnpass]]/[[WindHaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[Dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[Uma]].\n   100→\n   101→**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[WindHaven]].\n   102→\n   103→**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[Uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n   104→\n   105→**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n   106→\n   107→**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n   108→\n   109→**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Character Arc Themes\n   114→\n   115→### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n   116→\n   117→**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n   118→\n   119→[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n   120→\n   121→His rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n   122→\n   123→### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n   124→\n   125→**Question:** What makes me special?\n   126→\n   127→[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n   128→\n   129→### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n   130→\n   131→**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n   132→\n   133→[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n   134→\n   135→Foils to his pacifism:\n   136→- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n   137→- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n   138→- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n   139→- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## Player Background Tie-Ins\n   144→\n   145→### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n   146→- Has a yurt outside [[Hinderstap]] village in [[Monethern]]\n   147→- Sent by [[Lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n   148→- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n   149→- A portal/wound links [[Hinderstap]] to the [[Shadowfell]]\n   150→\n   151→### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n   152→- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n   153→- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n   154→- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n   155→- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n   156→- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[Uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→## Story Beats Still To Hit\n   161→\n   162→### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n   163→- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n   164→- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n   165→- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n   166→\n   167→### The Raven Queen's Transition\n   168→- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n   169→- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n   170→- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n   171→"}]},"uuid":"a0ee9c9a-defc-4053-aa11-a77549743e3b","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:41.948Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ec9b9fc9-3674-4d4c-b534-19561f28140c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"d5ac4aa4-b827-4a06-8dda-96689bd46858","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01P1tCdw3Zr6shyLSyupRzEw","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Diesa 'Daina' Fireforge\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Daina\", \"Daina Reborn\", \"The Forge\", \"The Force\"]\n     6→race: \"Hill Dwarf\"\n     7→class: \"Barbarian\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Magna Har]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Daina's Backstory\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Diesa \\\"Daina\\\" Fireforge/Daina Reborn.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Diesa \"Daina\" Fireforge is the party's unstoppable force -- a hill dwarf barbarian forged in exile and hardened by loss, who carries a lineage she does not understand and a rage she channels into protecting everyone she could not protect before. She is heir to the [[First Forge]] and the [[Bronzebeard]] dynasty, twin-soul of [[Bergs]], and the woman whose heartbeat commands warforged.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Reborn Form\n    23→The portrait shows Daina after her transformation -- **Daina Reborn**. Her skin has shifted to a **dark purple-grey** tone, striking and otherworldly. Her hair, once red like her father's, is now **white-platinum**, pulled back in a tight bun that emphasizes her fierce, angular features. Her eyes blaze with **glowing amber-orange** light -- no pupils visible, just radiance.\n    24→\n    25→She wears **heavy plate armor** of dark grey steel, covered in **glowing orange runic inscriptions** -- geometric patterns and dwarven symbols that pulse like a heartbeat. The armor has angular, segmented plates across the shoulders and chest, with **orange crystal gem inlays** set into key points of the breastplate and pauldrons. These runes are not decorative; they are the Fireforge legacy made visible, carved into her armor the way her father once carved weapons.\n    26→\n    27→She carries a **large warhammer or maul** over one shoulder, its head heavy and brutal, the handle wrapped in dark leather. The weapon's silhouette against the burning orange sky behind her creates an image of absolute martial authority.\n    28→\n    29→The overall effect is a forge at full heat -- dark metal glowing from within with contained power. She does not look peaceful. She looks like the moment before a hammer strikes an anvil.\n    30→\n    31→### Pre-Reborn (inferred)\n    32→Before the transformation, Daina was a red-haired hill dwarf woman, compact and coiled with power. Her father's greataxe was her primary weapon, and she carried the Fireforge heirloom -- a piece marking her family's ties to the [[Bronzebeard]] dynasty. She dressed practically in worn traveling clothes and armor, keeping an eye out always for a \"strange black-eyed red-bearded stranger.\"\n    33→\n    34→## Personality\n    35→\n    36→**ISTP (The Virtuoso)** -- Practical, action-oriented, emotionally guarded. Shows love through doing, not saying. The person who just throws herself up a tower because the math says it is faster than the stairs.\n    37→\n    38→**Motivations**: Find and destroy the black-eyed, red-bearded stranger who murdered her father. Understand what she is -- the Fireforge name, the heirloom, the warforged that respond to her heartbeat. Secretly, she wants a home.\n    39→\n    40→**Under pressure**: Becomes a weapon. The rage kicks in -- muscles swell, eyes narrow, breathing goes deep and rhythmic. After the Reborn transformation, her runes glow orange and she becomes genuinely terrifying: a platinum-haired dwarf wreathed in geometric fire.\n    41→\n    42→**Moral compass**: Operates on survival and reciprocity. Protects those who have earned protection and hunts those who have earned hunting. Would never betray kin. Would never let a child suffer what she suffered.\n    43→\n    44→## Backstory\n    45→\n    46→Daina was born in the [[Frozen Forest]] in the dead of winter. Her father, [[Thorkon Fireforge]], was once a royal weaponsmith of renown in [[Vag'Mahar]], married to Hlin. A rival weaponsmith, supported by one of the Bronzebeards, sabotaged Thorkon's weapon shipments by lacing them with deadly poison. When the Red Bronzebeard was affected, Thorkon was accused of attempted assassination and forced to flee.\n    47→\n    48→Hlin gave birth to Daina in the Frozen Forest and died of blood loss and cold twelve hours after naming her \"Diesa.\" Thorkon, devastated, changed his name to Tor Ironforge, renamed Diesa to Daina, and raised her in solitude. He traded with forest clans, forging weapons with deliberate defects to avoid recognition. He never forged fine weaponry again -- except for the greataxe he left to Daina.\n    49→\n    50→On his deathbed, poisoned by a mysterious visitor -- a red-bearded stranger with jet black eyes and a strange tattoo -- Thorkon bequeathed the greataxe and the Fireforge heirloom to Daina. She set out alone, wandering from town to town, hunting the stranger who killed her father.\n    51→\n    52→## Key Moments\n    53→\n    54→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    55→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    56→| 0 | Original intake; \"a furiously short person elbowing through crowds\" | Physically small but occupying space aggressively |\n    57→| 7 | Hits [[Dallor]] 5 times in one session | Establishing her method of conflict resolution |\n    58→| 19 | Uses soul bag containing children's souls for disguises | Moral weight -- pragmatism vs. horror |\n    59→| 23 | Charges a T-Rex face-first | Classic Daina: leaps before looking |\n    60→| 27 | Jumps off a 50ft cliff | Grabs [[Nocturnus Wayne]], screams \"slow fall!\" and rides him down |\n    61→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc | Identified as Fireforge by [[Ifrin the Pactbound]]; survives the phoenix's eruption |\n    62→| 100+ | Daina Reborn | Platinum hair, glowing runes, warforged commander. The Reborn transformation. |\n    63→| 130+ | Connects to six warforged simultaneously through her heartbeat | Stops being an avenger and becomes a leader |\n    64→\n    65→## Relationships\n    66→\n    67→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- Battle sisters. Brigit plans, Daina executes. The campaign's bedrock relationship.\n    68→- **[[Bergs]]** -- Twin-soul connection. Both Fireforges, both apprentice [[Magna Har]]. Their parallel arcs toward discovering their heritage is a major slow-burn revelation.\n    69→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Combat partners. Mutual respect expressed through violence. She grabs him, screams \"slow fall!\" and rides him down cliffs.\n    70→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** -- He casts Shield of Faith on her instinctively. She hits things that get near him.\n    71→- **[[Dallor]]** -- Daina hits Dallor. Repeatedly. This is not a relationship; it is a running gag with a body count.\n    72→- **[[Thorkon Fireforge]]** -- Her murdered father. The quest to avenge him expanded into the Fireforge dynasty revelation.\n    73→\n    74→## Fighting Style\n    75→\n    76→**Class**: Barbarian (Path of the Berserker / Ancestral Guardian).\n    77→\n    78→**Primary Stats**: STR > CON > DEX.\n    79→\n    80→**Weapons**: Greataxe (father's heirloom, later replaced/supplemented); warhammer/maul (Reborn form).\n    81→\n    82→**Signature abilities**:\n    83→- **Rage**: Literal barbarian rage. Muscles swell, eyes narrow. In Reborn form, runes glow orange.\n    84→- **Reckless Attack**: \"Just throws herself up a tower.\" No hesitation, maximum violence.\n    85→- **Warforged Command**: Her heartbeat syncs with warforged, visible as a shared orange pulse. She commands six simultaneously.\n    86→\n    87→**Combat philosophy**: Quick for a dwarf. Moves in bursts -- still, then sudden explosive action. She launches, leaps, charges. No hesitation. She is the party's id: pure action, raw emotion, unfiltered violence. But her rage is grief, and her violence is love expressed through protection.\n    88→\n    89→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    90→\n    91→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    92→\n    93→- **Race/Class**: Duergar (Dark Dwarf) Barbarian\n    94→- **Player**: Jasmine\n    95→- **Key Features**: Lava motif; ruby red eyes (from magic artifact); dark subtle facepaint; half-shaved lava-red hair with hottest parts fading white; glowing lava freckles and scars\n    96→- **Expression**: No-nonsense, first to stop shenanigans before they get off the rails\n    97→- **Equipment**:\n    98→  - [[Anchor of War]]: molten-edged axe with simple handle (lava aesthetic); she frequently uppercuts with it\n    99→  - [[Heart of the Forge|Heart of the Mountain]]: large fist-sized ruby embedded in center of breastplate\n   100→  - Soul bag from a night hag (zipper face -- stitched eyes and partially open mouth, witchy)\n   101→  - Bracers of defense\n   102→  - Conch shell\n   103→  - Amulet with zipper-mouth emblem\n   104→  - Multiple full bags of coins and a wineskin of ale\n   105→- **Visual contrast**: Lava/volcanic fire (contrasts with [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s forest/campfire fire)\n   106→- **Armor style**: Barbarian minimalist -- lighter shoulderpads/belt than typical plate\n   107→\n   108→## Gallery\n   109→\n   110→![[assets/pc-art-source/Diesa \"Daina\" Fireforge/Daina Reborn.jpg]]\n   111→"}]},"uuid":"69fa029b-a23d-4300-b67d-697b29cc2542","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:47.469Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d5ac4aa4-b827-4a06-8dda-96689bd46858","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"53d61eea-9998-4108-807b-5211431585c6","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01NYH9h8TZ66CXDtqAqfVgcD","toolUseID":"toolu_01NYH9h8TZ66CXDtqAqfVgcD","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:47.959Z","uuid":"3a6ff006-fe5d-4007-890f-8bdb8ee53751","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"ea31fdb2-eb4e-45c1-898f-7951ce569f9d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NYH9h8TZ66CXDtqAqfVgcD","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Sprocket Overspark\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Brain\", \"The Clock\", \"Sabine Ra's Chosen\"]\n     6→race: \"Gnome\"\n     7→class: \"Wizard (Divination)\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Alabaster Seat]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Character Sheets\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Sprocket Overspark/Sprocket_hq_mardov_bg.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Sprocket Overspark is the party's divination wizard -- a gnome who hides world-ending power behind a wide grin and mismatched boots. He is the vessel for [[Sabine Ra]], goddess of fate, carrier of the cloak of the [[Raven Queen]], and the living conduit between the world of the dead and the world of the living. He uses humor to deflect from the fact that he is carrying the weight of fate itself, and he builds Nocturnus-themed maid outfits with the same enthusiasm he brings to channeling cosmic necromancy.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A small **gnome** of diminutive stature, standing perhaps three feet tall with a wiry build and an oversized personality. His **wild grey-white hair** sprays outward in all directions, framing a face dominated by a **wide, gap-toothed grin**, a large bulbous nose, and bright, alert eyes behind **large round brass goggles** pushed up onto his forehead.\n    23→\n    24→He wears a **tall pointed yellow hat** that adds significant height to his silhouette. His outfit is a chaotic assemblage of colorful layers: a **red vest-jacket** with silver-grey metallic accents and buckles over a teal-green inner layer, secured with various straps and clasps. A **thick brown leather tool belt** wraps around his waist, heavy with pouches, vials, gears, and gadgets. His pants are **off-white or cream**, stuffed into **mismatched boots** -- one green with brass buckles, the other brown -- a detail that speaks volumes about his priorities (mechanisms over matching).\n    25→\n    26→His hands are covered in **brown leather work gloves**. He carries a **wooden staff topped with a blue crystal orb** that glows faintly with arcane energy. Behind him spread **golden crystalline geometric wings** -- translucent, mechanical, and beautiful, radiating light in sharp angular patterns. These wings are expressive: they flutter when he is excited, fold tight when scared, and spread wide for dramatic moments.\n    27→\n    28→The overall effect is part mad inventor, part court jester, part cosmic conduit -- exactly what Sprocket is.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ENTP (The Debater/Inventor)** -- Sprocket is the mad scientist with a heart. He argues, tinkers, experiments, and pushes boundaries. Beneath the chaos is someone who understands cosmic-level magic and uses pocket sand and simulacrums with equal enthusiasm.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Understand everything -- every mechanism, every spell, every cosmic secret. Keep his friends alive using increasingly improbable gnomish ingenuity. Secretly terrified of [[Sabine Ra]]'s cloak and what it means that it chose him.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes the smartest person in the room and stops pretending otherwise. The grin stays but the eyes go sharp. Casts from pure mathematical precision -- Disintegrate to center of mass, Hypnotic Pattern on exactly the right heads.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Casual, instinctive generosity wrapped in chaotic behavior. Gave a beggar genie two gold coins and was rewarded with a dragon ally. Would never experiment on the unwilling. Believes knowledge should be free and power should be earned.\n    39→\n    40→## Backstory\n    41→\n    42→Sprocket was marked for death and freed simultaneously by the [[Alabaster Seat]]. He is a divination wizard who knows his end is coming -- \"the ultimate answer to a question you ask yourself every fight.\" He carries a shard of a lich's soul in his pocket and hears [[Sabine Ra]]'s voice. His recklessness is not stupidity: it is the behavior of someone who knows exactly when and how they will die and is racing to accomplish everything before the clock runs out. His romantic interest in Trixie (a pink-haired gnome captured by hobgoblins) adds a personal stake to his cosmic burden.\n    43→\n    44→## Key Moments\n    45→\n    46→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    47→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    48→| 0 | Original [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] intake; arrives by carriage studying fate | Introduced apart from the group |\n    49→| 2 | Touches the Demi-Lich phylactery with mage hand | First contact with [[Sabine Ra]]; deals the first crack |\n    50→| 19 | Advocates leaving the tieflings behind | Reveals cold pragmatism that puts him at odds with [[Brigit Wildfire]] |\n    51→| 24 | Charges off to rescue Trixie from hobgoblins | \"Sprocket immediately picked up on the description of Trixie and charged off to the rescue!\" |\n    52→| 25 | Charges directly into caltrops | Classic Sprocket recklessness |\n    53→| 101 | Simultaneous Reverse Gravity cast with [[Brigit Wildfire]] | Redirect projectiles mid-flight; peak magical synergy |\n    54→| 130+ | Gains cloak of the [[Raven Queen]], channels necromancy | The gnome who makes maid outfits is now the conduit of cosmic fate |\n    55→| 136 | Anemone can't hold on; there won't be another alabaster seat | The joke stops being funny |\n    56→\n    57→## Relationships\n    58→\n    59→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- Big sister / little brother. She is exasperated by his chaos; he is grounded by her organization. Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] (Rabbit and Elk). Their fates are cosmically intertwined.\n    60→- **[[Bergs]]** -- The Buddy Cop Duo. Bergs is the muscle, Sprocket is the brain. Bergs's summary explicitly calls them \"buddy cop adventures.\"\n    61→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Sprocket builds simulacrum servants dressed as Nocturnus in frilly maid outfits. He also \"listens to the thoughts of Nightmare and finds nothing good.\" Comic antagonism masking genuine concern.\n    62→- **Trixie** -- Romantic interest. A pink-haired gnome whose capture sent Sprocket charging into a hobgoblin-infested tomb.\n    63→- **[[Sabine Ra]]** -- His patron/possessor. The goddess of fate chose him as vessel, and the cloak of the Raven Queen sits on his shoulders. He jokes about it because the alternative is screaming.\n    64→\n    65→## Fighting Style\n    66→\n    67→**Class**: Wizard (Divination), high level with access to 9th level spells.\n    68→\n    69→**Primary Stats**: INT (primary), DEX/CON (secondary).\n    70→\n    71→**Weapons**: Staff with blue crystal orb (arcane focus); pocket sand (legitimate tactical option).\n    72→\n    73→**Signature abilities**:\n    74→- **Portent Dice**: Divination wizard's core -- he knows the future and deploys foreknowledge at maximum impact moments.\n    75→- **Disintegrate**: Targeted to center of mass with mathematical precision.\n    76→- **Hypnotic Pattern**: Deployed on exactly the right targets.\n    77→- **Counterspell**: The party's anti-magic defense.\n    78→- **Mage Hand**: Used for everything -- retrieving torches, holding phylacteries to ceilings, picking locks from a safe distance.\n    79→- **Simulacrum**: Creates copies (often dressed inappropriately) for tactical purposes.\n    80→- **Reverse Gravity** (7th level): Coordinated with Brigit for devastating combined casting.\n    81→\n    82→**Combat philosophy**: The Swiss army knife. Sprocket does whatever the situation demands with the precision of someone who has already seen the outcome. When quiet and calculating, he is the most dangerous thing in the room. His crystalline wings and tiny stature make him easy to underestimate -- a mistake enemies make exactly once.\n    83→\n    84→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    85→\n    86→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    87→\n    88→- **Race/Class**: Goblin Wizard\n    89→- **Player**: The packrat magic item hoarder\n    90→- **Third Eye**: On forehead, sees into the ethereal plane. Normally not visible; shown via a subtle glowing \"20\" at the spot (can see the future and pick the number on the roll)\n    91→- **Hands**: 6 fingers\n    92→- **Goggles**: Large, with a possible Benjamin Franklin / National Treasure flip-down attachment\n    93→- **Magic Color**: Purple or ethereal light blue\n    94→- **Equipment**:\n    95→  - 3 spellbooks: one clearly evil (face on cover like Harry Potter monster book), one with Eye of [[Ioun]] symbol, one (thickest) with a wrench on it\n    96→  - Gaudy amethyst ring of ostentatious proportions\n    97→  - Silver braided circlet (on belt loop or head)\n    98→  - Boots of speed (gift from [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]])\n    99→  - Magic broom (broken in two pieces across his back)\n   100→- **Personality in art**: In the middle of explaining how his idea worked but cost minor collateral damage and it's okay because the client agreed to pick up the tab\n   101→- **Audrey's note**: \"Good lord, Sprocket needs to stop hoarding\"\n   102→\n   103→## Gallery\n   104→\n   105→![[assets/pc-art-source/Sprocket Overspark/Sprocket_hq_mardov_bg.png]]\n   106→"}]},"uuid":"242ca19e-4a18-423e-8de8-9cf4c3466431","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:47.959Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ea31fdb2-eb4e-45c1-898f-7951ce569f9d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"4db2a547-dede-4eff-b147-ce053641822c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E7FHBGwwp5dxqwLQAZnjRC","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Brigit Wildfire\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Wild Spirit of Fire\", \"The Heart\"]\n     6→race: \"Changeling/Shapeshifter\"\n     7→class: \"Druid (Circle of Wildfire) / Barbarian multiclass\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[The Distillery]], [[Good Guys Guild]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Liz, Ch. 0-136)\", \"Backstories\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigite Wildfire.jpg\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigit's Progenitor Form.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Brigit Wildfire is the emotional center of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] and the campaign's primary narrator, having authored 109 of the party's 176 chapter notes. She is a druid of wildfire renewal who channels the ancient power of the [[Stag Zodiac]], chosen vessel of the Progenitors, and custodian of the [[Anchor of Death]]. More than any other party member, Brigit is the one who remembers, who documents, and who refuses to let any moment be forgotten.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Normal Form\n    23→A young woman with vibrant red hair, partially shaved on one side, swept back and falling in loose waves. She has bright green eyes, fair skin dusted with freckles across her cheeks and nose, and slightly pointed ears hinting at her changeling nature. Her expression in repose is watchful and intelligent, lips slightly parted as though mid-thought.\n    24→\n    25→She wears a **green bodice** with decorative stitching along the neckline, laced up the front with brown leather cord over a darker undershirt. A **dark cloak** drapes over one shoulder, clasped at the throat with a simple metal pin. Her forearms are wrapped in **tan leather bracers** with visible stitching. A sturdy **leather belt** with a metal buckle sits at her waist. Her overall palette is forest greens, warm browns, and dark earth tones -- the colors of someone who lives closer to the woods than to any city.\n    26→\n    27→Her most striking visual feature is the **glowing molten fire orb** she conjures above her open palm -- a crackling sphere of orange-red magma and embers that illuminates her face from above, casting warm light across her freckled skin and catching the green of her eyes. This is her wildfire magic made manifest, and she holds it with casual familiarity.\n    28→\n    29→### Progenitor Form\n    30→A dramatic transformation into something ancient and otherworldly. In this form, Brigit appears as a **dark ethereal elven woman** with wild, flowing dark red-brown hair whipping upward as though caught in an invisible storm. Her ears are sharply pointed, her skin has shifted to a pale grey-blue tone, and her eyes blaze with pure glowing white light -- no pupils, no iris, only radiance.\n    31→\n    32→She wears **dark ornate armor** with metallic chains draped across her chest and shoulders. The most striking element is the **glowing red-orange energy** that cracks through her skin and armor like magma breaking through cooling stone -- luminous fissures running along her shoulders, arms, and chest. Embers and sparks trail from these cracks, drifting upward into darkness. A small amber pendant or jewel hangs from her neck. The overall effect is terrifying and beautiful: the freckled druid girl is gone, replaced by something primal, volcanic, and ancient.\n    33→\n    34→### Visual Evolution\n    35→Brigit's appearance charts her arc from uncertain young druid to Progenitor. Early campaign Brigit is the green-clad girl with the fire orb -- warm, approachable, grounded. Late campaign Brigit in Progenitor form is cold, alien, and wreathed in cracking light. The two forms share red hair and fierce eyes, but little else. This contrast is the visual story of the character.\n    36→\n    37→## Personality\n    38→\n    39→**ENFJ (The Protagonist)** -- Brigit is the emotional center of gravity. She organizes, documents, worries, and rallies. She leads not through authority but through sheer force of caring.\n    40→\n    41→**Motivations**: Protect her found family above all else. Understand and master the wild forces inside her -- the Stag, the Progenitor form, the zodiac connections. Secretly, she is terrified that the fire inside her is not renewal but destruction.\n    42→\n    43→**Coping mechanisms**: Brigit copes by doing. She writes notes obsessively, makes plans, skyrites warnings over towns. She processes emotion through transformation -- literally shifting into animals when overwhelmed. Her humor is dry, exasperated, and grounded. She is the \"mom friend\" who hides exhaustion behind competence.\n    44→\n    45→**Under pressure**: Frighteningly focused. The warmth drops out of her voice. She stops writing notes and starts giving orders. Embers trail from her hair. At peak stress she shifts into Lox or the Progenitor -- when she stops being Brigit entirely and becomes something ancient and terrifying.\n    46→\n    47→**Moral compass**: Will always protect innocents. Will never abandon a party member. Frames morality through renewal: fire that burns to let new growth emerge. She would burn a forest to save the people inside it.\n    48→\n    49→## Backstory\n    50→\n    51→Brigit was an orphaned druid from the wilds outside [[Windhaven]]. Her parents were killed by fearful villagers who did not understand her nature. She grew up trusting animals more than people, living on the edges of civilization. When the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] put out a call for recruits, she answered -- arriving at the town walls in wolf form before shifting to human, establishing her duality from the very first moment.\n    52→\n    53→## Key Moments\n    54→\n    55→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    56→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    57→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by [[Sabine Ra]] | Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    58→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | Party cohesion building |\n    59→| 13 | Liberation of [[Argos]] | First major quest completion |\n    60→| 28 | Vodram mission with [[Nyx]], [[Irethia]], [[Varis]] | Transforms into mice with Irethia; forges deep trust with the sub-group |\n    61→| 30-37 | Underdark descent | Party faces darkness together |\n    62→| 86 | The Reunion | Becomes a battlefield commander coordinating scattered party members across a continent |\n    63→| 100 | Shadowfell | Grapples with versions of herself that died or never existed |\n    64→| 119-125 | Time fractures | Confronts the revelation that she IS a Progenitor -- not just channeling one |\n    65→| 130 | Progenitor ritual in Eden with the [[Children of the Forest]] | Ascends the locket, gains custody of the [[Anchor of Death]] |\n    66→| 136 | Final battle | Loses her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle; must find her way forward |\n    67→\n    68→## Relationships\n    69→\n    70→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- Battle sisters. Brigit is cerebral and cautious; Daina is impulsive and violent. They complement each other perfectly. Brigit plans, Daina executes. The campaign's bedrock relationship.\n    71→- **[[Sprocket Overspark]]** -- Big sister / little brother energy. Brigit is exasperated by Sprocket's recklessness but deeply protective. They are the magical backbone of the party. Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] (Elk and Rabbit).\n    72→- **[[Nyx Khamestria]]** -- Fire and Darkness. Reluctant allies who became deeply loyal friends. A warlock of darkness and a druid of fire who shouldn't work together, but do.\n    73→- **[[Varis Laindon]]** -- The Forest Bond. Fellow protectors of the wild, cosmologically linked through the [[Stag Zodiac]] and [[Doe Zodiac]]. The campaign's most harmonious pairing.\n    74→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** -- Mutual respect between two protectors. Brigit's notes shift from discomfort with Stalwart's brutality to acceptance that he channels rage productively.\n    75→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- The party fought to save Nocturnus from the [[Nightmare Sword]] rather than abandon him. Brigit tried to polymorph him into a bunny. Uses Greater Restoration on him daily.\n    76→\n    77→## Fighting Style\n    78→\n    79→**Class**: Druid (Circle of Wildfire), high level with access to 7th+ level spells.\n    80→\n    81→**Primary weapon**: Magic itself -- she rarely swings a physical weapon.\n    82→\n    83→**Signature abilities**:\n    84→- **Wall of Fire**: Her go-to battlefield control. Uses fire as a barrier, not just damage.\n    85→- **Conjure Animals (Giant Spiders)**: Summons pairs to swarm enemies while she repositions.\n    86→- **Reverse Gravity**: Coordinated simultaneous casting with [[Sprocket Overspark]].\n    87→- **Polymorph**: Transforms herself into an Air Elemental for flight; transforms allies for tactical advantage.\n    88→- **Wild Shape**: Used for utility (bat for scouting, spider for infiltration, pangolin for healing, Stag for divine ceremony), not combat tanking.\n    89→- **Transport via Plants**: The party's primary long-range teleportation.\n    90→\n    91→**Combat philosophy**: Fluid, druidic. She moves like water around obstacles, then strikes like a crashing wave. In animal forms, she fights with genuine animal ferocity -- no human hesitation. She is the party's most versatile combatant, shifting between healer, controller, and damage dealer as the situation demands.\n    92→\n    93→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    94→\n    95→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    96→\n    97→- **Race/Class**: Human(?) Druid/Barbarian (\"Wild Spirit of Fire\")\n    98→- **Player**: Liz\n    99→- **Key Features**: Deep Irish folklore roots; Keyleth (Critical Role) as visual inspiration; facepaint and hair on fire first with body following (mid-transformation/rage); flaming stag horns\n   100→- **Fire type**: Forest/campfire fire (contrasts with [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s lava fire)\n   101→- **Equipment**:\n   102→  - [[Zodiac Locket|Anchor of Death]]: locket with zodiac symbology carved in -- zodiacs are owl, wolf, wolverine, serpent, bat, mongoose, pangolin, rabbit, turtle, fox, doe, and stag. Main structure is black and white stag horns forming the oval of the locket\n   103→  - Caduceus staff effigy tucked into belt loop\n   104→- **Art direction**: \"If her facepaint & hair were to be the part on fire first with some indication that the rest of the body were to join soon after, that's how I typically describe it.\"\n   105→- **Audrey's note**: \"I think Liz is going to love how she's built (adds to the barbarian nature)\"\n   106→\n   107→## Gallery\n   108→\n   109→![[assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigite Wildfire.jpg]]\n   110→![[assets/pc-art-source/Brigit/Brigit's Progenitor Form.jpg]]\n   111→"}]},"uuid":"8897c751-9c4d-463a-9370-9a0dbdb8bc70","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:48.279Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4db2a547-dede-4eff-b147-ce053641822c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
