{"parentUuid":"bdaee744-b1a2-4107-af5d-e56159cf797d","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XccAa3Z8RWj9WYGunYJwuf","toolUseID":"toolu_01XccAa3Z8RWj9WYGunYJwuf","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:49.164Z","uuid":"9e7c8b59-4d30-4dd0-aa18-438c161a75dd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"9e7c8b59-4d30-4dd0-aa18-438c161a75dd","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XccAa3Z8RWj9WYGunYJwuf","toolUseID":"toolu_01XccAa3Z8RWj9WYGunYJwuf","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:49.164Z","uuid":"56eb8ee5-0452-47db-b238-ccc15bffa0e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"bdaee744-b1a2-4107-af5d-e56159cf797d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XccAa3Z8RWj9WYGunYJwuf","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Varis Laindon\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Varis Liadon\", \"Knight of the Dusken Wood\", \"The Eyes\"]\n     6→race: \"Half-Elf\"\n     7→class: \"Ranger\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 6\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 6-136)\", \"Varis's Backstory\", \"Summary 28\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon.jpg\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon -- Knight of the Dusken Wood.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Varis Laindon is the party's sentinel -- a half-elf ranger who fights because he could not save his father, protects because he could not protect his village, and keeps watch because if he stops watching, it happens again. He arrived through a portal from [[Vodram]], an alternate reality destroyed by [[Vecna]], and carries that apocalyptic weight with quiet, devastating competence. His 200+ damage single-turn is the culmination of every sunrise he watched and every watch he kept.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Standard Form\n    23→A **muscular half-elf male** with **golden-brown shoulder-length hair** and slightly pointed ears marking his elven heritage. His face is ruggedly handsome with a **short beard and stubble**, strong jawline, and watchful brown eyes that never stop moving -- tracking, assessing, counting.\n    24→\n    25→He wears **brown leather armor** with ornate **gold-copper trim** -- scrollwork designs embossed into the chest piece and shoulder guards. **Fur-lined shoulders and cloak** give him a ranger's woodsman silhouette, warm and practical. Multiple **leather pouches** hang from his belt. His overall palette is earthy: brown leather, gold-copper accents, green forest behind him.\n    26→\n    27→He holds a **longbow** at the ready, with **glowing cyan-teal magical arrows** that emit an ethereal light. A full **quiver** is visible on his back. The cyan arrow glow is his visual signature -- when Varis draws his bow, cyan light reflects in his eyes and the world goes still.\n    28→\n    29→### Knight of the Dusken Wood Form\n    30→A weathered, grizzled transformation. His hair has shifted to **long silver-white**, his face more lined and hardened. He wears a **dark green-brown cloak** over leather armor, firing his longbow mid-combat in a **misty forest** setting. The palette shifts from warm golds to cool greens and greys. He moves like fog between trees -- visible, then not, then there is an arrow where you were standing.\n    31→\n    32→## Personality\n    33→\n    34→**ISTJ (The Logistician)** with a buried INFJ core. Varis presents as practical and duty-bound. He organizes defenses, commands guards, makes tactical assessments. But beneath the military bearing is someone who watches sunrises alone, carries a pseudo-dragon skull as a talisman, and dumps diamonds on dying strangers without being asked.\n    35→\n    36→**Motivations**: Protect the weak from those with power and without conscience. Find Yeldan -- or the truth of what happened to his father. Secretly, he wants to believe his father is alive.\n    37→\n    38→**Under pressure**: Becomes terrifyingly competent. Voice goes flat, movements become economical. Channels the Knight of the Dusken Wood -- silver-haired, battle-hardened, a figure out of legend.\n    39→\n    40→**Moral compass**: Hates petty politics with visceral fury. Will always fight for those \"screwed over by petty politics.\" Will break rules for righteousness but never for convenience.\n    41→\n    42→## Backstory\n    43→\n    44→Varis grew up at [[Tower Bridge]], a wilderness outpost near [[Dawnpass]]. His father Yeldan was a hunter, scout, and guide; his mother Lianala a druid. At age 95 (roughly 17 in human years), they hunted a pack of pseudo-dragons together. He kept the skull of his first kill.\n    45→\n    46→Two weeks later, Yeldan spotted undead scouts. The local lord believed him but could not convince others in time. Two more weeks, and the army arrived -- zombies, skeletons, wraiths, mummies, led by the lich [[Ned'zailok]]. Varis and his father volunteered for the militia.\n    47→\n    48→They held for a week. Yeldan was sent as the fastest scout to request aid. The militia broke a day later. The [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] arrived -- too late to hold, fast enough to cover retreat. Most civilians escaped. The militia took massive losses. Yeldan was never found.\n    49→\n    50→STB commander [[Dockson]] shamed the lord into acting. Varis enlisted with the STB, fighting alongside them for eighteen months until Ned'zailok was defeated. He has been protecting the weak and keeping an ear out for the lich ever since.\n    51→\n    52→## Key Moments\n    53→\n    54→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    55→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    56→| 6 | Appears through a portal from [[Vodram]] | A member of the STB from an alternate reality destroyed by Vecna |\n    57→| 28 | Vodram mission with [[Brigit Wildfire]], [[Nyx Khamestria]], [[Irethia]] | \"These people from Sabine's world... they got dropped into the worst place possible, and never complained\" |\n    58→| 28 | Kills Roderick in cold blood; Brigit sees and still trusts him | The moment Varis learned that trust can survive witnessing violence |\n    59→| 29 | Secret collaboration with Brigit to find hobgoblin Grinda | Brigit covers for him in wolf form |\n    60→| 100+ | Commands city guards, inspires former-drab to take up arms | Grows from ranger following orders to commander giving them |\n    61→| 136 | Fires the shot that reverberates through a god's body | 200+ damage. The culmination of every sunrise he watched. |\n    62→\n    63→## Relationships\n    64→\n    65→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- The Forest Bond. Fellow protectors of the wild, cosmologically linked through [[Stag Zodiac]] and [[Doe Zodiac]]. The campaign's most harmonious pairing.\n    66→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Varis brought Nocturnus back from death with a diamond. Unspoken bond.\n    67→- **[[Sprocket Overspark]]** -- Both assigned to the [[Forest Zenith]] with Brigit. Elk, Doe, Rabbit.\n    68→- **[[Irethia]]** -- Vodram mission squad. Mutual respect forged through extreme danger in another plane.\n    69→- **Yeldan** -- His father. Never found. Varis stopped expecting to hear news but never stopped hoping.\n    70→- **[[Ned'zailok]]** -- The lich who destroyed his home. Can never be certain a lich is gone.\n    71→\n    72→## Fighting Style\n    73→\n    74→**Class**: Ranger (Hunter).\n    75→\n    76→**Primary Stats**: DEX > WIS > CON.\n    77→\n    78→**Weapons**: Longbow with magical cyan-teal arrows (primary); short sword (backup).\n    79→\n    80→**Signature abilities**:\n    81→- **The Draw**: His signature moment. When Varis pulls his bow, everything slows. Breathing steadies, pupils contract, body perfectly aligned. Cyan glow reflects in his eyes. Release. Arrow flies true.\n    82→- **Multi-Attack**: In sustained combat, fires volleys of arrows with devastating speed.\n    83→- **Colossus Slayer / Hunter's Mark**: Bonus damage that stacks to 200+ in critical moments.\n    84→- **Natural Explorer**: Moves through forests like fog between trees.\n    85→\n    86→**Combat philosophy**: Military precision. He sees the battlefield from above (metaphorically), identifies targets, and calls them. His fighting style is vigilance weaponized -- every watch he kept, every sunrise he guarded, condensed into the moment the arrow leaves the string.\n    87→\n    88→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    89→\n    90→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    91→\n    92→- **Race/Class**: Elf Ranger\n    93→- **Player**: Scott\n    94→- **Key Features**: Classic elf ranger in garb and build; does NOT have long flowing blonde hair -- instead has straw yellow/red hair cropped into a practical cut; exposed forearm with stag horn tattoos up and down it like vines (tattoo transforms into his bow)\n    95→- **Equipment**:\n    96→  - Tattoo-turned-bow with lightning motif, glows electric blue\n    97→  - Breastplate made of bronze dragon scales\n    98→  - Ring of air elemental command\n    99→- **Art direction**: \"Scott always scoffs at the pictures I show him that have the long flowing blonde hair\"\n   100→\n   101→## Gallery\n   102→\n   103→![[assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon.jpg]]\n   104→![[assets/pc-art-source/Varis/Varis Laindon -- Knight of the Dusken Wood.jpg]]\n   105→"}]},"uuid":"e0ecb0cf-96b6-47d9-b820-766c8899e099","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:49.164Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bdaee744-b1a2-4107-af5d-e56159cf797d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"e0ecb0cf-96b6-47d9-b820-766c8899e099","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_015CY28JmVJyP4xrzTVnZiEx","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/characters/pcs/Nocturnus Wayne.md"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":13100,"cache_read_input_tokens":26147,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":13100,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9gbq5J5eMNKT8xM2rQ9","type":"assistant","uuid":"a5a4512a-a815-42e7-b550-79ad3f6b45e9","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.636Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"a5a4512a-a815-42e7-b550-79ad3f6b45e9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","toolUseID":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.639Z","uuid":"14d6d7d4-8845-4525-8812-4995248c5027","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"14d6d7d4-8845-4525-8812-4995248c5027","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","toolUseID":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.639Z","uuid":"ebf3cfbb-d125-4e1a-96a7-575b499e97c6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"a5a4512a-a815-42e7-b550-79ad3f6b45e9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XmRTEQSUcKvsc9XbWxY1ET","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Nocturnus Wayne\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Loner\", \"The Exile\", \"Darkest Knight\"]\n     6→race: \"Drow\"\n     7→class: \"Monk / Vampire\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive (undead)\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Summary 23\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Nocturnus/Nocturnus Wayne -- Post Vampire.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Nocturnus Wayne is the party's dark knight -- a drow monk turned vampire who kills gods because someone has to, and because the darkness in him is already paid for. He operates alone by preference and in the dark by necessity. His quest to destroy his aunt [[Oblivion Wayne]], the drow vampire queen, expanded into a cosmic role as godslayer and shadow champion. He is the party member who scares everyone and makes everyone laugh, often simultaneously.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **tall, lean male** with **deathly pale grey-white skin** that reads as corpselike in any lighting. His **long white-platinum hair** falls past his shoulders in lank, straight strands, framing a face with **dark, sunken predatory eyes** set deep in shadowed sockets. His features are gaunt, angular, and aristocratic in the way that death is aristocratic.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **black spiked armor** covered in organic, thorn-like blade protrusions -- the armor looks less forged and more grown, as though darkness itself crystallized into protective plate. Dark leather and metal layers are secured with multiple **buckles and straps**, creating a silhouette of controlled menace. Behind his shoulders, **wing-like blade protrusions** extend upward -- not wings he can fly with, but decorative barbs of dark metal that echo a bat's wingspan.\n    25→\n    26→In his right hand he grips a **greatsword** -- his companion weapon, Bane -- with casual, intimate familiarity. His left hand drips with **fresh blood**, the only warm color in his entire palette. The blood catches light that his skin does not.\n    27→\n    28→The color palette is intentional: cool blue-grey, black armor, white hair, and the singular shock of red blood. Every element reads gothic, vampiric, and dangerous. He is the visual embodiment of the party's darkest capabilities.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**INTJ (The Architect)** -- Strategic, brooding, relentlessly goal-oriented. He has the emotional range of a gothic cathedral: vast, echoing, mostly in shadow, with occasional shafts of light that are more striking for their rarity.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Fulfill the prophecy -- kill the gods that need killing. Master the vampire within. Secretly, he wants to be human again. Not the power, not the immortality -- just the warmth.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes the thing that monsters are afraid of. His pale skin goes translucent, his movements blur, his blade drinks blood in a \"dusty, molasses pull.\" He fights with Bane in a partnership more intimate than any living relationship. Uses Empty Body to become untouchable.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Follows a code that exists entirely in his own head. Chose not to win when winning would cost more than losing. Will never turn another person. Will never feed on the unwilling.\n    39→\n    40→His Summary 23 worldview: \"It becomes evident that I'll have to save these fools. Again.\"\n    41→\n    42→## Backstory\n    43→\n    44→Nocturnus is a drow nobleman whose aunt, [[Oblivion Wayne]], is the leader of the Drow and a vampire queen. She married [[Ebon Wayne]], a vampire drow who captured the [[Bat Zodiac]] and uses it to create alpha vampires. Oblivion turned her own kin to create an army of loyal vampires, using each turned individual's innate loyalty to depose her brother and unite the drow.\n    45→\n    46→Nocturnus was turned against his will. He carries the vampirism as both curse and weapon, seeking vengeance against his aunt while struggling with the hunger inside him. His acquisition of the sentient evil sword [[Nightmare]] nearly consumed him -- the party had to physically restrain him (Brigit tried to polymorph him into a bunny, Sylvia stunned him) before he could be fitted with a \"pacifying spore\" from the mushroom Sovereign covering half his face.\n    47→\n    48→## Key Moments\n    49→\n    50→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    51→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    52→| 0 | Original intake; appears from nowhere in combat | Established as the one who is already there when you notice him |\n    53→| 8 | Party encounters drow forces | His heritage becomes relevant; drow warlock kills Nyx's owl |\n    54→| 23 | Summary: \"I'll have to save these fools. Again.\" | Defines his self-image as reluctant protector |\n    55→| 27 | Uses Slow Fall to save [[Daina Fireforge]] | Despite his \"I save these fools\" attitude, he repeatedly protects others |\n    56→| 36 | Defeats cousin Duskin; absorbs Nightmare sword fragment | The corruption deepens |\n    57→| 38 | Kills hags the party was trying to save | \"Our resident evildoer decides to kill the evil hags. We tried to stop him.\" Party restrains him. |\n    58→| 100+ | Pacifying spore covers half his face | Visible metaphor: literally half himself, half something darker |\n    59→| 136 | Absorbs fragment of Asmodeus's essence | \"I FEEL LIKE A GOD.\" Crystalline heart, power of a dead god. Party: \"Nocturnous, you good bro?\" |\n    60→\n    61→## Relationships\n    62→\n    63→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** -- Two loners who respect competence but disagree on methods. Light paladin vs. dark knight. The Nightmare Sword arc forced confrontation.\n    64→- **[[Varis Laindon]]** -- Unspoken bond. Varis brought Nocturnus back from death with a diamond. They do not discuss it. They do not need to.\n    65→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- Combat partners. She grabs him and screams \"slow fall!\" and rides him down cliffs.\n    66→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- She fights for his soul. Greater Restoration daily. Tried to polymorph him into a bunny. This is love expressed as persistent magical intervention.\n    67→- **Bane** -- His sentient greatsword. Animate the sword as a partner, not a tool. Blood flows UP the blade. When he talks to Bane, his hand relaxes on the hilt like holding hands.\n    68→- **[[Oblivion Wayne]]** -- His aunt. Drow vampire queen. His prophesied target.\n    69→\n    70→## Fighting Style\n    71→\n    72→**Class**: Monk (Way of Shadow) / Vampire abilities.\n    73→\n    74→**Weapons**: Greatsword (Bane); unarmed strikes; the darkness itself.\n    75→\n    76→**Signature abilities**:\n    77→- **Empty Body**: Becomes translucent, untouchable. In this form he looks young -- like the man he was before the turning.\n    78→- **Slow Fall**: Saves allies from fall damage (notably Daina off cliffs).\n    79→- **Vampiric Drain**: Blood flows in \"dusty, molasses pull\" toward his blade.\n    80→- **Shadow Step**: He does not walk into rooms. He is already there when you notice him.\n    81→- **Pocket Sand**: A legitimate tactical option he uses to sneak into people's rooms and throw at them.\n    82→\n    83→**Combat philosophy**: Inhuman fluidity. He does not walk, he glides. He does not turn, he pivots. His hair moves half a second behind his head, like underwater. His blade is an extension of his arm. He fights with Bane as a partner -- the most dangerous two-person unit in the party, except one of them is a sword.\n    84→\n    85→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    86→\n    87→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    88→\n    89→- **Race/Class**: Edgelord vampire aesthetic\n    90→- **Player**: Ben\n    91→- **Key Features**: Kingdom Hearts Nobody / Hot Topic circa MCR outfit; chains and zippers everywhere; doesn't wear traditional armor; scarring\n    92→- **Eyes**: Jade green on the side holding the sword, blood red in the other\n    93→- **Equipment**:\n    94→  - Sword devoid of light and shine (\"almost the way that suede doesn't reflect light\")\n    95→  - Scabbard with jade inlays; sword formerly named \"Envy\"\n    96→- **Art direction**: \"I wanna embarrass him in the most lovingly badass way\"; \"DLC for Kingdom Hearts\"\n    97→- **Audrey's note**: \"Ah yes, the edgelord\"\n    98→\n    99→## Gallery\n   100→\n   101→![[assets/pc-art-source/Nocturnus/Nocturnus Wayne -- Post Vampire.jpg]]\n   102→"}]},"uuid":"20cd43fa-8609-40d7-bd9e-d14e15d52b0f","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.639Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a5a4512a-a815-42e7-b550-79ad3f6b45e9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"20cd43fa-8609-40d7-bd9e-d14e15d52b0f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_015CY28JmVJyP4xrzTVnZiEx","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/characters/pcs/Nyx Khamestria.md"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":13100,"cache_read_input_tokens":26147,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":13100,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9gbq5J5eMNKT8xM2rQ9","type":"assistant","uuid":"fdb4b447-6f15-405c-abab-c81044ef1057","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.908Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"fdb4b447-6f15-405c-abab-c81044ef1057","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","toolUseID":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.926Z","uuid":"c49a3a00-1bd8-4b38-86f8-0740ed72b04f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"c49a3a00-1bd8-4b38-86f8-0740ed72b04f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","toolUseID":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.927Z","uuid":"90413597-882f-449d-918b-ed39960f95c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"fdb4b447-6f15-405c-abab-c81044ef1057","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q6ESQ2oS3MsxnfPmmjYPGG","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Nyx Khamestria\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Knight of the Flower Field\", \"The Shadow\", \"The Wildcard\"]\n     6→race: \"Tiefling\"\n     7→class: \"Warlock\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Nyx's Backstory\", \"Summary 46\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Nyx/Nyx Khamestria.jpg\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Nyx/Nyx Khamestria, Knight of the Flower Field.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Nyx Khamestria is the unwanted twin -- a tiefling warlock who was sold before she was born, rejected by both buyer and seller, and who found power in being the one nobody planned for. She is the party's wildcard: chaos engine, lateral thinker, and the person who convinces enemies to throw away their swords mid-fight. Her quest to save her sister [[Xyn]] from the archfey [[Reynardine]] drives everything she does.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→### Portrait Form\n    23→A striking **tiefling woman** rendered in shades of purple and violet. Her most prominent feature is her **voluminous curly dark purple-violet hair**, thick and luxurious, cascading around her face and shoulders in heavy ringlets that seem to catch and hold starlight. Two small **curved horns** emerge from her hair like ram's horns, their surface smooth and pearlescent.\n    24→\n    25→Her skin is **light pink-fair**, her features soft and youthful with full lips and dark, melancholic eyes that hold intelligence and sadness in equal measure. Her expression is guarded -- arms crossed, chin slightly down, the posture of someone who learned to be small.\n    26→\n    27→She wears a **white button-up blouse** with a small collar, tied at the throat with a **dark purple ribbon**. Over this she wears a **purple blazer jacket** in a rich violet tone. The overall aesthetic is unexpectedly modern and scholarly -- more bookish student than demon-touched warlock. This disconnect between her appearance and her nature is the character in a single image.\n    28→\n    29→### Knight of the Flower Field Form\n    30→A complete visual inversion. In this persona, Nyx appears as a **cheerful, whimsical witch girl** with curly **pink-light hair** and small horns peeking out from beneath a **wide-brimmed blue witch hat** decorated with charms. Her skin is **rosy pink**, her expression open and laughing -- the opposite of her portrait's guardedness.\n    31→\n    32→She wears a **blue cloak** over a **white blouse** and **dark skirt**, with **blue lace-up boots**. A **pink tail** curls playfully behind her. She rides a **wooden broomstick** through the sky, one hand raised in a wave, her **owl companion** (Hedwig) perched on the broomstick beside her. Green bead charms dangle from the broom handle.\n    33→\n    34→The Knight of the Flower Field is the life Nyx would have lived if she had been loved. The contrast between this bright, joyful persona and her true guarded self is heartbreaking.\n    35→\n    36→## Personality\n    37→\n    38→**INFP (The Mediator)** -- Idealistic, deeply feeling, and perpetually at war with a world that told her she was a mistake. She processes everything internally. Her melancholy is not performative; it is the natural state of someone who learned as a child that her parents considered her a \"failed experiment.\"\n    39→\n    40→**Motivations**: Save her sister [[Xyn]] from [[Reynardine]]. Prove that being the \"wrong twin\" does not make her wrong. Secretly, she wants her parents to see what she has become and regret everything.\n    41→\n    42→**Under pressure**: Her defiance surfaces. The shy posture straightens. Warlock powers manifest as fey energy crackling along her horns and tail. Her voice drops and becomes eerily calm. She is most dangerous when most still.\n    43→\n    44→**Moral compass**: Would never do to anyone what was done to her. Would never sell a child, never reject someone for failing to be useful. Extends empathy to monsters because she knows what it feels like to be called one.\n    45→\n    46→## Backstory\n    47→\n    48→Two thousand years ago, [[Tygras]] made a deal with an archfey from [[Vecna]]'s plane -- [[Reynardine]], the Madness Bringer -- exchanging the secret of channeling pure arcana for a portal between worlds.\n    49→\n    50→Twenty-six years before the campaign, Nyx's parents made a deal with the same fey, unknowingly. In exchange for the philosopher's stone (the ability to channel pure arcana), they agreed to offer a child as vessel. That child was actually a twin-soul -- both tieflings, both fey-touched.\n    51→\n    52→Eight years before the campaign, a twist of fate revealed that the dominant twin (Nyx) was not the vessel Reynardine needed. Instead, her sister (dormant until then) was taken. Nyx received the patron's powers as a consolation, the deal was revealed, and her parents -- who believed the deal had gone sour -- spurned her as a failed experiment.\n    53→\n    54→Seven years before the campaign, Reynardine returned. [[Xyn]] was born as a separate being -- the perfect vessel, easily manipulated after nineteen years as a dormant passenger. Reynardine's plan: obtain the [[Diadem of Dawn]], use Xyn to open a portal to the fae, merge the planes, and allow [[V-Mordred]] entrance to the realm.\n    55→\n    56→Nyx's Summary 46 reveals the full depth of her internal struggle: she has pieced together the cosmic puzzle of her patrons ([[Gabriel]], [[Juliannia]], Reynardine, [[Graz'zt]]) and made a leap of faith -- trusting Reynardine because she believes in the good person he once was. \"I hope you can forgive me.\"\n    57→\n    58→> **CRITICAL NOTE**: [[Reynaldine]] (Fall Feylord) and [[Reynardine]] (Nyx's patron, the Madness Bringer) are distinct entities.\n    59→\n    60→## Key Moments\n    61→\n    62→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    63→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    64→| 0 | Original intake; introduced scribbling secrets in a candlelit inn room | Established as interior, secretive |\n    65→| 3-4 | Lights braziers impulsively, causing undead swarms | Early chaos; party frustration |\n    66→| 8 | Owl (Hedwig) murdered by drow warlock | \"Nyx's beloved owl then died a quick and painless death, shrouded in glory and triumph.\" Deeply sarcastic, deeply sad. |\n    67→| 19 | Accepts Revenant soul-glamour disguise | The screaming voice in her head: \"REVENGE. KILL THE GNOME.\" |\n    68→| 28 | Vodram mission; destroys Eye of Vecna's head with twin fire beams | Definitive \"we work together\" moment with [[Brigit Wildfire]] |\n    69→| 46 | Final summary: addresses the party about patrons and the amulet | \"I hope you can forgive me\" -- the campaign's most psychologically complex character moment |\n    70→\n    71→## Relationships\n    72→\n    73→- **[[Tesca]]** -- The Tiefling Sisters. Both tieflings in a world that hangs tieflings for practicing magic. Instant kinship through shared persecution. Both assigned to the [[Harvest Zenith]] alongside [[Irethia]].\n    74→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- Fire and Darkness. Reluctant allies who became deeply loyal. Nyx's chaos and Brigit's order create a balance neither expected.\n    75→- **[[Xyn]]** -- Her twin sister, the \"correct\" vessel, trapped by [[Reynardine]]. Saving Xyn is Nyx's reason for everything.\n    76→- **[[Reynardine]]** -- Her patron, her abuser, the being who holds her sister. Accepting his power feels like proving her parents right.\n    77→- **[[Irethia]]** -- Prophetically linked through the Harvest Zenith. Owl, Fox, Mouse.\n    78→- **Hedwig** -- Her owl familiar. Murdered in Ch. 8. The loss was devastating.\n    79→\n    80→## Fighting Style\n    81→\n    82→**Class**: Warlock (Archfey patron -- [[Reynardine]]).\n    83→\n    84→**Signature abilities**:\n    85→- **Eldritch Blast**: Primary ranged attack, twin beams of fey energy.\n    86→- **Invisibility**: Makes allies invisible for stealth operations.\n    87→- **Fey magic**: Involuntary-looking -- purple-green light bleeds from her eyes, fingertips, and the cracks in her composure.\n    88→- **Persuasion mid-combat**: Convinced an enemy to toss his darkness sword away in the middle of battle.\n    89→\n    90→**Combat philosophy**: Lateral thinking that looks reckless but is often brilliant. She solves problems from angles nobody else considers. Her magic manifests as something leaking out rather than being cast -- involuntary, fey, and unsettling.\n    91→\n    92→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    93→\n    94→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    95→\n    96→- **Race/Class**: Tiefling Witch (pinkish)\n    97→- **Player**: Lillian\n    98→- **Key Features**: Pinkish skin; pink/white hair (not too pink -- \"pinkish red\"); curly horns poking through witch hat; tail\n    99→- **Equipment**:\n   100→  - Witch hat (mandatory -- \"hands down *needs* a witches hat\")\n   101→  - Harry Potter style robe/cloak over the back, dipping down beneath magic broom\n   102→  - Glowing emerald pendant holding her cloak (belonged to her sister [[Xyn]] -- the color contrast against pink indicates this)\n   103→  - Magic broom\n   104→- **Position**: Background with [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] and [[Randal]]\n   105→- **Note**: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] bequeathed their magic items for the group before leaving (departed characters)\n   106→\n   107→## Gallery\n   108→\n   109→![[assets/pc-art-source/Nyx/Nyx Khamestria.jpg]]\n   110→![[assets/pc-art-source/Nyx/Nyx Khamestria, Knight of the Flower Field.png]]\n   111→"}]},"uuid":"78c7187b-2fae-4943-9264-2b6c306b6c81","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:50.926Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fdb4b447-6f15-405c-abab-c81044ef1057","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"08be536a-c6af-45fd-9b45-684d45cd2ac0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_018kapdr2vPtzakGU7KQkgY9","toolUseID":"toolu_018kapdr2vPtzakGU7KQkgY9","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:55.654Z","uuid":"f99f5044-e26c-4516-b156-735781ebe439","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"75432db9-c907-4a77-b16c-15cd64f07b93","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018kapdr2vPtzakGU7KQkgY9","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Stalwart Little Goldentooth\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Stalwart\", \"Lit of the Summerborn\", \"The Anchor\", \"The Shield\"]\n     6→race: \"Gold Dragonborn\"\n     7→class: \"Paladin\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Bahamut]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"DM Notes\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little.png\", \"assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little Goldentooth -- Lit of the Summerborn.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Stalwart Little Goldentooth is the self-appointed leader of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] -- a title nobody contests because he is an enormous golden dragonborn paladin who will argue until everyone gives up. He is the party's anchor: righteous, stubborn, divinely empowered, and capable of channeling divine magic without a deity through pure conviction. His personal quest to avenge his brother Stewart's death expanded into a cosmic role linked to the [[Pestilence Zenith]] at [[Bynar]].\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **gold-scaled dragonborn** of imposing stature, standing well above most humanoids. His scales are a warm, burnished gold that catch and reflect light, giving him a naturally regal presence. His head features a rounded snout with small facial horns, and his eyes are a piercing yellow-amber. His clawed gold feet and thick tail complete the draconic silhouette.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **gleaming silver plate armor** with ornate gold filigree -- swirling decorative patterns etched into the pauldrons, breastplate, greaves, and gauntlets. The armor is pristine and well-maintained (he polishes it obsessively). A **crimson cloth tabard or sash** is draped around his neck and across one shoulder, providing a bold warm accent against the silver steel. His belt is dark leather with a purple-toned buckle.\n    25→\n    26→In his right hand he wields a **longsword** with a gold crossguard and red inlaid detail, its blade long and polished. In his left he carries a large **kite shield** -- silver-grey steel decorated with elegant gold filigree scrollwork. The shield is as much a character as a weapon; it faces threats before Stalwart consciously does.\n    27→\n    28→His overall silhouette reads as classic paladin -- armored, shielded, upright, commanding. The gold scales against silver armor create a visual metaphor: he is both the treasure and the vault that protects it.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ESTJ (The Executive)** with a deep ISFP wound. Stalwart presents as the classic paladin -- righteous, organized, certain. But his obsession with retribution masks a person who has been deeply hurt by injustice and is terrified of being powerless again.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Deliver divine retribution justly and completely. Prove that faith without a deity is the purest form of belief. Secretly, he is still searching for Stewart, and the search has become less about finding his friend and more about proving that loyalty means something.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Stops arguing and starts swinging. His morningstar becomes a metronome of justice. He shouts battle commands that are half tactics and half sermon. His faith manifests as radiant energy pulsing from his shield in concentric waves.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Believes in cosmic justice -- wrong must be answered, evil must be punished, scales must balance. Struggles with mercy. His Shield of Retribution is both his weapon and his worldview: when struck, strike back. He is learning, slowly and painfully, that retribution and redemption can coexist.\n    39→\n    40→## Backstory\n    41→\n    42→Stalwart grew up at the Little Orphanage, raised by Gran alongside his brothers, including Stewart. When goblins attacked the [[Sunless Citadel]] and killed Stewart, Stalwart's life gained a singular purpose: find the goblins responsible and deliver justice. He was a sailor before the brigade, an orphan before that, and a paladin of [[Bahamut]] in his soul from the very beginning. His name -- \"Little\" -- is a source of tension for a dragonborn who stands tallest in any room.\n    43→\n    44→## Key Moments\n    45→\n    46→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    47→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    48→| 0 | Original [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] intake | Immediately assumes command; nobody stops him |\n    49→| 0-1 | Speaks Draconic to kobold swarm | His heritage carries authority; the kobolds part like a tide |\n    50→| 2 | Dallor's betrayal | Establishes the party norm: betrayal is not forgiven |\n    51→| 6 | \"Light vs Dark\" competition vs [[Dallor]] | Punches Dallor; catches something out of his hand |\n    52→| 23 | Casts Shield of Faith on [[Daina Fireforge]] vs T-Rex | \"Just in time, because the T-Rex bites down on Daina\" |\n    53→| 24 | Carves messages into hobgoblin prisoner | Defines his duality: protector and punisher |\n    54→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc | Feywilde courts, faith tested by captivity |\n    55→| 100+ | Discovers deity-free divine magic | Shield of Retribution + Heart of the Faith = pure conviction made manifest |\n    56→| 136 | Time fractures | \"Looks like he's seeing ghosts\" and only \"kind of remembers\" fighting alongside the party |\n    57→\n    58→## Relationships\n    59→\n    60→- **[[Dallor]]** -- Natural enemies. Stalwart is the moral arbiter; Dallor the opportunist. The Betrayal in Ch. 2 was never forgiven.\n    61→- **[[Nocturnus Wayne]]** -- Two loners who respect each other's competence. Stalwart is order and justice; Nocturnus is vengeance and darkness. The Nightmare Sword arc forced the party to choose Nocturnus's soul over safety.\n    62→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- Mutual respect between two protectors. Brigit chronicles; Stalwart acts.\n    63→- **[[Tiberius D Rex]]** -- The Dragonborn Brotherhood. Both devout followers of [[Bahamut]], both loud, both proud. Tiberius is the comic relief version of Stalwart's righteous fury.\n    64→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- He casts Shield of Faith on her instinctively. His protective instinct extends to everyone, but Daina draws it out most reliably.\n    65→- **Linda** (the chicken) -- His emotional support animal. He carries her everywhere and refuses to leave her behind.\n    66→\n    67→## Fighting Style\n    68→\n    69→**Class**: Paladin (faith without a specific deity -- pure conviction).\n    70→\n    71→**Weapons**: Longsword and kite shield (primary); spiked morningstar (referenced in some sources).\n    72→\n    73→**Signature abilities**:\n    74→- **Shield of Faith**: Cast reflexively to protect allies in danger.\n    75→- **Shield of Retribution**: When struck, strikes back -- radiant energy pulses from the shield.\n    76→- **Divine Sense**: Used constantly to detect evil.\n    77→- **Lay on Hands**: Healing through faith.\n    78→- **Draconic Heritage**: Speaks Draconic with natural authority; can command kobolds and other draconic creatures through lineage.\n    79→\n    80→**Combat philosophy**: Heavy, committed strikes. Every swing is a judgment. He does not dodge -- he blocks with the shield and punishes the attacker. His fighting style is a sermon delivered in steel. He leads from the front, always, and positions himself between danger and his party.\n    81→\n    82→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    83→\n    84→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    85→\n    86→- **Race/Class**: Golden Dragonborn Paladin in plate armor\n    87→- **Key Features**: Golden scales; plate armor with helmet; scaled skin exposed at feet/underarms\n    88→- **Equipment**:\n    89→  - Shield with shoe symbol (nursery rhyme \"The Little Old Lady Who Lived in a Shoe\" -- his surname is \"Little\")\n    90→- **Position**: Background with [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]]\n    91→- **Note**: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] bequeathed their magic items for the group before leaving (departed characters)\n    92→\n    93→## Gallery\n    94→\n    95→![[assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little.png]]\n    96→![[assets/pc-art-source/Stalwart/Stalwart Little Goldentooth -- Lit of the Summerborn.png]]\n    97→"}]},"uuid":"3d2c8a2c-1ea6-40b7-8eef-1aa7f9e82293","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:55.654Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"75432db9-c907-4a77-b16c-15cd64f07b93","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

Needs enrichment from source material.\n    14→"}]},"uuid":"0d4997ba-7386-46f4-bb16-880fdb330361","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:56.529Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e3786a77-85de-4543-a50e-214c9a71bcf1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"5a9d6238-0b53-4db8-bfef-68a533034ad9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01GbeTwTh7aV64DbseLbvS1S","toolUseID":"toolu_01GbeTwTh7aV64DbseLbvS1S","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:56.847Z","uuid":"e340501d-3c90-4d93-8dde-857c08836f24","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"5a9d6238-0b53-4db8-bfef-68a533034ad9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GbeTwTh7aV64DbseLbvS1S","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Randal\"\n     5→aliases: [\"The Diplomatic Bard\", \"The Mediator\"]\n     6→race: \"Half-Elf\"\n     7→class: \"Bard / Warlock\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive (dying)\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 10\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 10-136)\", \"Summary 21\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Randal/Randal.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Randal is the party's smoothest operator -- a bard who watches instead of performing, using music as cover for something deeper. He sings, charms, dispels, and inspires. Where [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]] leads through force and [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigit]] through knowledge, Randal leads through charisma. He is dying, and the awareness that every day slips away gives him an urgency that manifests as gentle wisdom rather than panic.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **young man** with **tan-olive skin** and slightly pointed ears suggesting half-elf heritage. His **messy dark brown-black hair** is tousled and unkempt, with slight stubble on his jaw. His expression is alert and watchful, eyes scanning the room even mid-performance.\n    23→\n    24→He wears a **green and brown patterned cloak-poncho** with spiral and geometric designs over dark inner layers in brown-maroon tones. His legs are wrapped in **cloth bindings** above simple **sandal-boots**. A **backpack** sits on his shoulders and a **short blade** hangs at his hip.\n    25→\n    26→He carries a **tall wooden staff** with an ornate **glowing lantern** hanging from its top, casting warm amber light. The lantern swings with his walking rhythm -- his personal lighthouse.\n    27→\n    28→The overall effect is traveler, not warrior. Earth tones, warm lantern glow, muted palette. He looks like someone who belongs on the road, moving between towns, earning his keep before accepting charity.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ISFP (The Adventurer)** -- Observant, adaptable, more comfortable blending in than standing out, despite being on stage. The music is not the point.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Find the [[Sunless Citadel]] and the [[Anchor of Pestilence]]. Earn his place through observation and timing. Secretly, he is watching someone specific in every crowd.\n    35→\n    36→**Moral compass**: \"There's something to be said for the power of ignorance... TRUTH beyond violence.\" His search continues. Every day is another that slips away. Death is closing in.\n    37→\n    38→## Key Moments\n    39→\n    40→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    41→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    42→| 10 | Arrives with [[Tesca]], delivered by cloaked figures | Mysterious entrance |\n    43→| 21 | Passes Grimnak's warning to party members without alerting \"Sally\" | The party's intelligence officer |\n    44→| 30 | Plays counter-charm for late-comers; inspires [[Daina Fireforge]] with \"a grunt and a nod\" | His music wakes Brigit from a mind-flayer stun |\n    45→| 32-33 | Pocket dimension with [[Daina Fireforge]] and [[Tiberius D Rex]] | Reveals dry humor and genuine affection |\n    46→\n    47→## Relationships\n    48→\n    49→- **[[Tesca]]** -- Arrived together. Fellow outsiders with hidden depths.\n    50→- **[[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]]** and **[[Dallor]]** -- All three assigned to the [[Winter Zenith]] (Pestilence). Serpent, Turtle, Mongoose.\n    51→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- His epic bard song wakes her from mind-flayer stun. Music as salvation.\n    52→- **[[Nyx Khamestria]]** -- He passes intelligence without alerting her; demonstrates his role as covert operator.\n    53→\n    54→## Visual Description (Session 100 Commission)\n    55→\n    56→*From the Audrey commission for \"Mardov: Echoes of the Shadowfell\" (Session 100, April 2022)*\n    57→\n    58→- **Race/Class**: Human Bard / God of Choice\n    59→- **Player**: Zach (character 1 of 2)\n    60→- **Key Features**: Unassuming, chill dude; PACSun model look; \"low key, no trips to the tailor, just off the rack\"; easy-going nature\n    61→- **Equipment**:\n    62→  - Panflute attached via leather cord\n    63→  - Staff\n    64→  - Shackles connected to nothing (dimensional ankle monitor)\n    65→  - Gauntlets (never shows his hands, okay if not pictured)\n    66→- **Lore**: God of Choice; lent his form to a friend who uses it to live out dreams of being in charge. Party suspects a [[Randal]] at the Bard college may or may not be the same guy. Having one side more divine than the other would be a cool visual nod.\n    67→- **Position**: Background\n    68→\n    69→## Gallery\n    70→\n    71→![[assets/pc-art-source/Randal/Randal.jpg]]\n    72→"}]},"uuid":"8831ab6e-703a-4d5b-a863-27d6b57a73d6","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:56.847Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a9d6238-0b53-4db8-bfef-68a533034ad9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"0f1ec095-360a-49da-90f1-4eac7d9c3ab9","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_014gVsHDFMAcGcBuEjY9aHpi","toolUseID":"toolu_014gVsHDFMAcGcBuEjY9aHpi","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:57.171Z","uuid":"8a91e222-8e21-4c38-80f1-0171e25ddf6d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"7d6cf473-f677-4c2c-bd9a-4fb8823cc7d6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014gVsHDFMAcGcBuEjY9aHpi","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Tezka Duskhallow\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Tesca\", \"The Scrapper\"]\n     6→race: \"Tiefling\"\n     7→class: \"Monk\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 10\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 10-136)\", \"Tesca's Backstory\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Tesca/Tesca at Twilight.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Tezka \"Tesca\" Duskhallow is a street kid in a monk's body -- she learned discipline from the monastery but her soul was forged in the slums. A tiefling who handles her heritage with defiance rather than shame, she is the party's scrapper: fast, fearless, and allergic to hypocrisy. Her quest leads to the [[Diadem of Dawn]] -- the very artifact that got her parents killed.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **lean, athletic tiefling woman** with **purple-violet skin** that catches light with an inner luminescence. Her build is clearly that of a trained fighter -- toned, functional muscle with a low center of gravity, always balanced on the balls of her feet.\n    23→\n    24→Her **lavender-white hair** is pulled back and gathered into a single **long braid** that falls well past her waist, ending in a **decorative tassel**. Two small **curved horns** rise from the top of her head like a natural crown -- she never tries to hide them. A **pointed, spaded tail** extends behind her, used consciously as a tool for balance during kicks and climbing.\n    25→\n    26→She wears a **purple-blue crop top** that leaves her midriff exposed, showing defined abdominal muscles. Her arms are wrapped differently: one forearm is covered in **cloth wraps**, while the other bears a **silver armband**. Her **loose flowing purple-indigo harem pants** are gathered at the ankles with dark fabric, cinched at the waist with a **blue sash-belt** whose ends trail behind her. A **simple necklace with a pendant** hangs at her throat. She wears **dark purple slippers** -- flexible, silent, perfect for a monk's footwork.\n    27→\n    28→The overall silhouette is flowing and graceful -- the billowing pants and trailing sash create movement even when she is still. Her color palette is entirely purple-violet-indigo-lavender, with blue accents. She looks like twilight given form.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ESTP (The Entrepreneur)** -- Action-first, instinct-driven, allergic to hypocrisy. She thinks with her body before her mind. Charming in a rough, unpolished way -- the kind of person who picks your pocket while making you laugh.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Experience the world her mentor described. Prove that a tiefling street urchin can be as worthy as any temple-raised acolyte. Secretly, she wants to find [[Elder Roku]] and thank him. She has never thanked anyone.\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes pure movement. Her monk training takes over -- flows between targets like water. She is most dangerous when silent, because Tesca is usually talking.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Hates bullies. Steals from those who have too much and gives to those who have too little (sometimes keeps some). Would never betray someone who shared their food with her.\n    39→\n    40→## Backstory\n    41→\n    42→Tezka was born to tiefling parents [[Tenzin Duskhallow|Tenzin]] and [[Kiera Duskhallow|Kiera]] in the slums of [[Dawnpass]]. The family was desperate, which led to crime. Her parents took a job from the Dusker's Initiative to break into the [[Monastery of the Dawn]], find the office of [[Elder Roku]], and locate clues to the [[Diadem of Dawn]].\n    43→\n    44→They were caught by Elder Roku, who showed mercy. He convinced the family to seek refuge in the monastery. But the parents were sloppy -- the Dusker's Initiative discovered the fake location they provided and ordered the \"loose ends tied up.\" The family's house was set ablaze. Tenzin was killed first, telling Kiera to run. Kiera doubled back toward the burning building with young Tesca in tow.\n    45→\n    46→Tesca survived and lived on the streets for years, thieving from merchants until a visiting monk spotted her. He offered safety at the monastery. She accepted warily. There she was teased and outcasted but trained, channeling her frustration into fighting under the elder monk's personal guidance. She retained her \"propensity towards light crime\" -- pulling pranks and stealing extra food.\n    47→\n    48→When her mentor died of old age, Tesca was wrongly accused of stealing offerings and vandalism. The monks threw her out. She took it as an opportunity to travel, seeking the places her mentor had described.\n    49→\n    50→The discovery that the Diadem of Dawn is actually the [[Anchor of Famine]] -- an artifact granting one wish at the cost of all spellcasting ability -- and that her parents died trying to find it, transformed her from someone running from her past to someone running toward the truth.\n    51→\n    52→## Key Moments\n    53→\n    54→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    55→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    56→| 10 | Arrives with [[Randal]], delivered by cloaked figures with a note and payment | Mysterious entrance |\n    57→| 19 | Accepts chicken-soul glamour disguise | Both she and [[Nyx Khamestria]] must maintain false identities to survive |\n    58→| 29 | Burns off mushroom hat successfully | Different response to magical coercion vs. Nyx's psychological fragility |\n    59→| 100+ | Discovers Diadem of Dawn = Anchor of Famine | Her parents' deaths connect to the central plot |\n    60→| 136 | Must wield the Diadem to destroy [[Tygras]]'s fae machine | Sacrificing magical abilities permanently. A monk who fights with her body accepts this differently than a wizard. |\n    61→\n    62→## Relationships\n    63→\n    64→- **[[Nyx Khamestria]]** -- The Tiefling Sisters. Both tieflings in a world that hangs them for magic. Two responses to oppression: Nyx through cunning and pacts, Tesca through discipline and patience. Both assigned to [[Harvest Zenith]] alongside [[Irethia]].\n    65→- **[[Randal]]** -- They arrived together. Both outsiders, both with hidden depths.\n    66→- **[[Irethia]]** -- Prophetically linked through the Harvest Zenith. Fox, Owl, Mouse.\n    67→- **[[Elder Roku]]** -- The monk who saved her parents and later trained her (through his monastery). She wants to find him and thank him.\n    68→- **[[Tenzin Duskhallow]]** and **[[Kiera Duskhallow]]** -- Her murdered parents. Their crime and death led to her life.\n    69→\n    70→## Fighting Style\n    71→\n    72→**Class**: Monk (Way of the Open Hand / Shadow).\n    73→\n    74→**Primary Stats**: DEX > WIS > CON.\n    75→\n    76→**Weapons**: Unarmed strikes, fists, feet, horns, tail.\n    77→\n    78→**Signature abilities**:\n    79→- **Flurry of Blows**: Continuous, flowing strikes -- kicks, punches, elbow strikes, headbutts, tail sweeps.\n    80→- **Ki-Powered Strikes**: Contact points glow briefly with faint violet light.\n    81→- **Stunning Strike**: Precise pressure-point attacks.\n    82→- **Patient Defense / Step of the Wind**: Evasion and mobility that makes her nearly untouchable.\n    83→- **Tail Mastery**: Used consciously for balance during kicks, wrapping objects when climbing, sweeping legs.\n    84→\n    85→**Combat philosophy**: She fights like a dancer who learned violence -- every strike flows into the next. Fluid, continuous, beautiful. She never stops moving. Her breathing stays controlled even as she shatters bones. Silence is her tell -- Tesca is usually talking, and when she stops, bones break.\n    86→\n    87→## Gallery\n    88→\n    89→![[assets/pc-art-source/Tesca/Tesca at Twilight.png]]\n    90→"}]},"uuid":"ff05b49c-296f-4d80-a0e6-d0606a8369d4","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:57.171Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7d6cf473-f677-4c2c-bd9a-4fb8823cc7d6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"c12b9857-4391-4263-be00-96dd95131dad","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_015Ne7Ui9dV5erw35Ghd1xaZ","toolUseID":"toolu_015Ne7Ui9dV5erw35Ghd1xaZ","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:57.458Z","uuid":"03a06ac4-ece1-44cc-b3a4-55d6cf1d48f5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
{"parentUuid":"f1dd5575-8a38-4540-9dd0-b82958fd60a4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015Ne7Ui9dV5erw35Ghd1xaZ","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Birt\"\n     5→aliases: [\"True Birt\"]\n     6→race: \"Aarakocra\"\n     7→class: \"Wizard\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Mardov (traveling)\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Mid-campaign\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 49-136)\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Birt/Birt.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Birt is a bird who chose to be wise instead of free -- a powerful high-level wizard wrapped in feathers, carrying humor as his shield and knowledge as his weapon. He deflects the weight of his power with jokes and teleports in and out of existence with deliberately comedic timing.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **humanoid aarakocra** with an eagle-like head featuring sharp eyes and a curved beak. His plumage is striking: **crimson and dark blue** primary feathers with **cream-white** accents. His flight feathers bear distinctive **red eye-spot markings**. His lower body features **scaled bird legs** ending in **taloned feet**.\n    23→\n    24→He wears **dark red-maroon vestments** trimmed with **gold** -- chevron patterns along the edges with gold dots and studs. The robes are formal, almost priestly, contrasting with his avian nature. Perched on his wing-hand is a **white dove companion**, providing visual contrast to his crimson plumage -- calm when Birt is excited, fluttering when Birt is calm.\n    25→\n    26→## Personality\n    27→\n    28→**INTP (The Logician)** with a comedic streak. The party's high-level spellcaster who deflects the weight of power with humor. Smarter than he lets on, funnier than he needs to be.\n    29→\n    30→**Motivations**: Master magic. Protect the phoenix. Secretly, he misses flying just to fly, without purpose or destination.\n    31→\n    32→**Under pressure**: Stops joking and starts casting. High-level wizard magic deployed with avian precision -- he sees the battlefield from above and drops spells like a raptor drops on prey.\n    33→\n    34→## Key Moments\n    35→\n    36→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    37→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    38→| 100+ | Blinded [[Dallor]] the Titan | High-level wizard deployed against cosmic threats |\n    39→| 117 | Healed the phoenix through feather magic | His unique responsibility |\n    40→\n    41→## Fighting Style\n    42→\n    43→**Class**: Wizard (high level, access to 8th-9th level spells).\n    44→\n    45→**Signature abilities**: Teleportation (blip in/out leaving a scatter of crimson feathers), blindness/light manipulation, simulacrums, phoenix healing through feather magic.\n    46→\n    47→**Combat philosophy**: Birdlike precision from elevation. He drops spells like a raptor drops on prey. When the comedy evaporates, what remains is devastating.\n    48→\n    49→## Gallery\n    50→\n    51→![[assets/pc-art-source/Birt/Birt.png]]\n    52→"}]},"uuid":"205b9e81-ccfe-4acb-b49d-cce3362f977c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:32:57.458Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f1dd5575-8a38-4540-9dd0-b82958fd60a4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"301a9432-56c9-4e3c-b2d7-791f4594f579","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0116NdShiBqe3f7nAsj3qTVe","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Barnabus 'Bergs' Fireforge\"\n     5→aliases: [\"Bergs\", \"Barnabus Blunderbeat\", \"The Newest God\"]\n     6→race: \"Hill Dwarf\"\n     7→class: \"Fighter / Myconid Sovereign / God\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], [[Artificer's Guild]], [[Magna Har]]\"\n     9→location: \"[[Feywilde]] (Sovereign)\"\n    10→status: alive (ascended)\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes (Ch. 0-136)\", \"Summary 36\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Bergs/Barnabus \\\"Bergs\\\" Fireforge.jpg\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Barnabus \"Bergs\" Fireforge is a dwarf who became a god and never stopped being a dwarf. He forges connections the way his bloodline forges metal, and he would rather make a friend than win a fight. He ascended to become the sovereign of the [[Feywilde]] -- simultaneously the newest god and the Demon Prince of Pleasure -- carrying all of these contradictions with the same steady hands that always forged and fought.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **stocky, broad-shouldered hill dwarf** with a completely **bald head** and a face dominated by a **massive bushy red beard** -- a truly magnificent facial mane that is a character unto itself. The beard features **multiple thick braids** secured with **metal clasps and rings**, the braids hanging well past his chest. The rest of the beard is full and wild, red-brown and meticulously maintained despite its volume.\n    23→\n    24→His face is **weathered and stern** with deep-set eyes beneath heavy brows, a prominent nose, and the hardened expression of someone who has worked with fire and metal his entire life. There is warmth there, but you have to earn it.\n    25→\n    26→He wears **heavy scale mail armor** -- overlapping metal scales in dark grey-silver tones. **Studded leather pauldrons** protect his shoulders, reinforced with **dark metal shoulder plates** studded with rivets. The armor is functional, well-worn, and practical -- no ornamentation, no gold trim. This is a working dwarf's armor, made for survival.\n    27→\n    28→After ascension, the visual shifts subtly: a faint warmth enters rooms with him, mushroom spores drift in his wake like tiny constellations, and his beard braids pulse with faint bioluminescent light. He still looks like a dwarf. The godhood makes that ordinariness feel startling.\n    29→\n    30→## Personality\n    31→\n    32→**ISFJ (The Defender) evolving into ENFJ**. Bergs starts as the quiet, loyal protector who acts through his hands. As the campaign progresses and he ascends to godhood, his sphere of care expands from \"my people\" to \"all people,\" but he never loses the fundamental dwarven groundedness.\n    33→\n    34→**Motivations**: Protect the network -- first the myconid network, then the party, then all of Mardov. Forge something that matters. Secretly, he wants to be enough: \"I may be the newest god, but I am enough.\"\n    35→\n    36→**Under pressure**: Becomes the immovable object. Voice drops lower, movements slow down, plants his feet. He anchors. In extreme moments, connects to the full network and speaks with the weight of every mind linked to his.\n    37→\n    38→**Moral compass**: Tried to feed a mushroom spore to Tiamat's heads to redeem them rather than kill them. Will always try diplomacy first, connection second, violence last. Gave himself to the Stone Table to save everyone. No one gets abandoned.\n    39→\n    40→## Backstory\n    41→\n    42→Bergs was planted by the [[Artificer's Guild]] among the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] as a covert agent. His true heritage as a Fireforge was unknown even to himself. Abandoned by his parents and raised by wolves, he was \"roped into this strange Maghar Dwarven mumbo-jumbo\" as the campaign revealed his connection to the [[Magna Har]] and the [[First Forge]].\n    43→\n    44→Through the campaign, he died and was resurrected by the myconid network, becoming something more than dwarf -- a living node in a fungal consciousness. He carried [[Kelemtor]] (a talking skull) as a partner. He discovered his twin-soul connection with [[Daina Fireforge]], learned he was heir to the First Forge, and ultimately sacrificed himself on the Stone Table to become sovereign of the Feywilde.\n    45→\n    46→## Key Moments\n    47→\n    48→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    49→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    50→| 0 | Original intake; covertly planted by [[Artificer's Guild]] | His true purpose hidden from the party |\n    51→| 24 | Throws [[Sprocket Overspark]] across a ravine with a rope | Peak buddy cop energy |\n    52→| 36 | Summary: \"buddy cop adventures\" with Sprocket | Defines the partnership |\n    53→| 36 | Sporepocalypse theory | Becomes convinced mushroom people will end the world; the party thinks he is insane |\n    54→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc | Identified as Fireforge by [[Ifrin the Pactbound]]; trades his maul |\n    55→| 100+ | Dies and is resurrected by myconid network | Becomes a living node in fungal consciousness |\n    56→| 130+ | Sacrifices himself on the Stone Table | Becomes sovereign of the [[Feywilde]], god of connection, Demon Prince of Pleasure |\n    57→\n    58→## Relationships\n    59→\n    60→- **[[Daina Fireforge]]** -- Twin-soul connection. Both Fireforges, both apprentice [[Magna Har]] (Daina the axe, Bergs the hammer). The slow-burn revelation of their shared heritage.\n    61→- **[[Sprocket Overspark]]** -- The Buddy Cop Duo. \"Not looking too good is Sprocket, who got fwoooph'd by a spider. Someone ought to heal that guy so I don't have to find someone else to go on buddy cop adventures with.\"\n    62→- **[[Kelemtor]]** -- A talking skull carried on his belt. They are a buddy comedy about death and connection. The skull narrates everything and judges lovingly.\n    63→- **[[Brigit Wildfire]]** -- She is the heart, he is the anchor. Both protect, but through different elements.\n    64→- **The Myconid Network** -- His resurrection source and extended consciousness. When connections drop, he panics.\n    65→\n    66→## Fighting Style\n    67→\n    68→**Class**: Fighter (Champion/Battle Master) evolving into divine combatant.\n    69→\n    70→**Weapons**: Maul (traded to Ifrin); post-ascension weapon Peacemaker (hovers above him, radiating light).\n    71→\n    72→**Signature abilities**:\n    73→- **Network Connection**: Pings connected minds like checking a phone; speaks with the weight of every linked consciousness.\n    74→- **Forging**: Uses blacksmith precision in combat -- every swing is a practiced strike.\n    75→- **Myconid Spores**: Post-resurrection, deploys fungal abilities for communication and control.\n    76→- **Divine Presence**: Post-ascension, light bends around him, spores drift, warmth enters with him.\n    77→\n    78→**Combat philosophy**: Economical, brutal, efficient. Every swing is a blacksmith's practiced strike. After ascension, his weapon Peacemaker hovers above him radiating light. He still fights like a dwarf, which makes the godhood feel startling.\n    79→\n    80→## Gallery\n    81→\n    82→![[assets/pc-art-source/Bergs/Barnabus \"Bergs\" Fireforge.jpg]]\n    83→"}]},"uuid":"eeeeaf91-e7ae-4d70-a27d-6faa282747f9","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:33:02.152Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"301a9432-56c9-4e3c-b2d7-791f4594f579","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"78590787-ea90-48c6-ba5e-42beb0ddc61a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"08565186-7602-4066-bdfe-ecc1a6a04372","agentId":"a8bfbbbb3719cc3f4","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012CeicCoLnwHDvpWsxagt3b","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: pc\n     4→name: \"Frozen Song\"\n     5→aliases: []\n     6→race: \"Dragonborn (Purple/Blue)\"\n     7→class: \"Bard\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\"\n     9→location: \"Unknown\"\n    10→status: unknown\n    11→first_appearance: \"Mid-campaign\"\n    12→source: [\"Player Notes\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/pc-art-source/Frozen Song/Frozen Song.png\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→A dragon who chose the lute over the lance. Frozen Song is a dragonborn bard whose name is a paradox -- frozen things do not sing, songs do not freeze -- and whose entire existence is about reconciling contradictions. Purple dragonborn are imposing; bards are gentle. He holds both without contradiction.\n    19→\n    20→## Appearance & Costume\n    21→\n    22→A **blue-purple scaled dragonborn** with a **rounded, horned head** featuring **multiple horn spines** extending backward from the skull like a natural crown. His scales are **purple-lavender** with a lighter underbelly. The horns vibrate slightly when he plays, adding natural resonance to his music.\n    23→\n    24→He wears a **simple dark blue-purple robe** with **white sleeves**, tied at the waist with a cloth belt. The simplicity of his clothing contrasts with the dramatic nature of his draconic form. He holds a **wooden lute** in playing position, fingers on the strings. The background is a **warm sunset**, orange and amber against his cool purple scales.\n    25→\n    26→## Personality\n    27→\n    28→**INFP (The Mediator)** -- Contemplative, artistic, at peace with paradox. He seeks the song that his name promises -- the one that exists at the intersection of ice and melody.\n    29→\n    30→When he plays, his eyes half-close and his scales soften in color. The music manifests as frost patterns in the air that melt as the notes fade.\n    31→\n    32→## Gallery\n    33→\n    34→![[assets/pc-art-source/Frozen Song/Frozen Song.png]]\n    35→"}]},"uuid":"816ff1f3-4944-4205-992b-6dcc70fe44c1","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:33:12.949Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"78590787-ea90-48c6-ba5e-42beb0ddc61a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}
